&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：对 alpha_sweep_a060_s43_fp32 的 gen_0-9 数据做 D105 MMCT 5-method 分析

### 背景
- exp_id: `alpha_sweep_a060_s43_fp32`
- 服务器: westd-17855
- 结果目录: `/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/alpha_sweep/a060_s43_fp32/`
- 状态: 训练在 gen_10 合成阶段 timeout (EXIT_CODE=124)，gen_0-9 全部完整
- **用 gen_0-9 数据做分析（T=10 时间点）**，不等 gen_10

### 关键对比
- parent: `alpha_sweep_a060_s42_fp32_v3` (s42) → PermG fwd=3/8, K≥3(3σ)=?
- α=0.50 s42: PermG=6/8
- 问题：α=0.60 s42 PermG=3/8 比 α=0.50 的 6/8 低 → 疑似非单调性
- 如果 s43 PermG ≤3/8 → 非单调性确认（系统性现象）
- 如果 s43 PermG >3/8 → s42 可能是单 seed 噪声

### 分析要求

1. **SSH 到 westd-17855**（ssh_read_only）确认 gen_0-9 目录完整：
   ```bash
   ls -d /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/alpha_sweep/a060_s43_fp32/gen_*/
   ```

2. **运行 D105 标准 MMCT 分析**（ssh_execute）：
   - 找到项目中的分析脚本（`analyze_mmct.py` 或 `run_mmct_analysis.py` 或类似）
   - 运行 5-method 分析，参数：
     - 8-pair: canary={token_entropy, ece} × downstream={d1, d2, d3, mauve}
     - n_perm=5000
     - 3σ 阈值
     - fdr_permethod（BH 校正）
   - 如果脚本自动检测 generation 数量，确认它用 gen_0-9（10 个时间点）
   - 如果脚本需要手动指定，传参限制到 gen_0-9

3. **收集完整结果**：
   - PermG fwd/rev (FDR 校正后)
   - K≥4(3σ), K≥3(3σ)
   - threshold_onset(3σ)
   - naive_xcorr, diff_xcorr
   - TY Granger
   - 各 pair 的详细结果（哪些 pair 显著）
   - drift percentages: token_entropy, distinct_1/2/3, ece, perplexity, mauve, effective_rank

4. **更新 registry**：
   - `update_registry(exp_id="alpha_sweep_a060_s43_fp32", field="metrics", value={...})` 包含所有上述指标
   - 在 metrics 中加注 `"note": "T=10 (gen_0-9, gen_10 timeout), partial completion"`
   - 设置 `checkpoint_path` 和 `log_path`

5. **输出对比表**（供 Director 决策）：
   ```
   | 指标 | s42 (α=0.60) | s43 (α=0.60) | α=0.50 s42 |
   | PermG fwd | 3/8 | ?/8 | 6/8 |
   | K≥3(3σ) | ? | ? | 6/8 |
   ```

### 项目路径
- 代码: `/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/`
- 环境: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`

runs 5-method lead-lag\\nanalysis on all canary-diversity pairs, computes K>=4 consensus, and optionally\\nruns full MMCT if null directories are provided.\\n\\nUsage:\\n    python batch_analysis.py --exp_dir results/alpha_sweep/a010_s43_bf16\\n    python batch_analysis.py --exp_dir results/alpha_sweep/a010_s43_bf16 \\\\\\n        --null_dirs results/alpha_sweep/a000_s42 results/alpha_sweep/a000_s43\\n\\nInput:  {exp_dir}/all_metrics.json\\nOutput: {exp_dir}/analysis_results.json (or --output path)\\n\\nDependencies: numpy, scipy, statsmodels\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport traceback\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom statsmodels.tsa.stattools import adfuller\\n\\nN_PERM = 5000\\nALPHA_SIG = 0.05\\n\\nCANARIES = [\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"ece\\\"]\\nDOWNSTREAMS = [\\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\", \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"mauve\\\"]\\n\\nHIGHER_IS_WORSE = {\\\"ece\\\", \\\"perplexity\\\"}\\nLOWER_IS_WORSE = {\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\", \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"mauve\\\"}\\n\\nALL_METRICS = [\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"ece\\\", \\\"perplexity\\\", \\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\",\\n               \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"effective_rank\\\", \\\"mauve\\\"]\\n\\n\\ndef load_series(data_dir):\\n    path = os.path.join(data_dir, \\\"all_metrics.json\\\")\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n    data.sort(key=lambda x: x[\\\"generation\\\"])\\n    out = {}\\n    for k in ALL_METRICS:\\n        vals = []\\n        for d in data:\\n            v = d.get(k)\\n            vals.append(v if v is not None else np.nan)\\n        out[k] = np.array(vals, dtype=float)\\n    return out, len(data)\\n\\n\\ndef orient(series, name):\\n    return -series.copy() if name in LOWER_IS_WORSE else series.copy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_drift(series_dict, n_gens):\\n    drift = {}\\n    for k in ALL_METRICS:\\n        s = series_dict.get(k)\\n        if s is None or len(s) < 2:\\n            drift[k] = None\\n            continue\\n        v0, v_last = s[0], s[-1]\\n        if abs(v0) < 1e-12:\\n            drift[k] = None\\n        else:\\n            drift[k] = round(float((v_last - v0) / abs(v0) * 100), 2)\\n    return drift\\n\\n\\ndef bh_fdr(p_values, alpha=0.05):\\n    pv = np.array(p_values, dtype=float)\\n    n = len(pv)\\n    valid = ~np.isnan(pv)\\n    adjusted = np.full(n, np.nan)\\n    if valid.sum() == 0:\\n        return adjusted.tolist(), [False] * n\\n    idx = np.where(valid)[0]\\n    p_valid = pv[idx]\\n    sorted_idx = np.argsort(p_valid)\\n    ranks = np.empty_like(sorted_idx)\\n statsmodels\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport traceback\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom statsmodels.tsa.stattools import adfuller\\n\\nN_PERM = 5000\\nALPHA_SIG = 0.05\\n\\nCANARIES = [\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"ece\\\"]\\nDOWNSTREAMS = [\\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\", \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"mauve\\\"]\\n\\nHIGHER_IS_WORSE = {\\\"ece\\\", \\\"perplexity\\\"}\\nLOWER_IS_WORSE = {\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\", \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"mauve\\\"}\\n\\nALL_METRICS = [\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"ece\\\", \\\"perplexity\\\", \\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\",\\n               \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"effective_rank\\\", \\\"mauve\\\"]\\n\\n\\ndef load_series(data_dir):\\n    path = os.path.join(data_dir, \\\"all_metrics.json\\\")\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n    data.sort(key=lambda x: x[\\\"generation\\\"])\\n    out = {}\\n    for k in ALL_METRICS:\\n        vals = []\\n        for d in data:\\n            v = d.get(k)\\n            vals.append(v if v is not None else np.nan)\\n        out[k] = np.array(vals, dtype=float)\\n    return out, len(data)\\n\\n\\ndef orient(series, name):\\n    return -series.copy() if name in LOWER_IS_WORSE else series.copy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_drift(series_dict, n_gens):\\n    drift = {}\\n    for k in ALL_METRICS:\\n        s = series_dict.get(k)\\n        if s is None or len(s) < 2:\\n            drift[k] = None\\n            continue\\n        v0, v_last = s[0], s[-1]\\n        if abs(v0) < 1e-12:\\n            drift[k] = None\\n        else:\\n            drift[k] = round(float((v_last - v0) / abs(v0) * 100), 2)\\n    return drift\\n\\n\\ndef bh_fdr(p_values, alpha=0.05):\\n    pv = np.array(p_values, dtype=float)\\n    n = len(pv)\\n    valid = ~np.isnan(pv)\\n    adjusted = np.full(n, np.nan)\\n    if valid.sum() == 0:\\n        return adjusted.tolist(), [False] * n\\n    idx = np.where(valid)[0]\\n    p_valid = pv[idx]\\n    sorted_idx = np.argsort(p_valid)\\n    ranks = np.empty_like(sorted_idx)\\n    ranks[sorted_idx] = np.arange(1, len(p_valid) + 1)\\n    m = len(p_valid)\\n    adj = p_valid * m / ranks\\n    adj_sorted = adj[sorted_idx].copy()\\n    for i in range(m - 2, -1, -1):\\n        adj_sorted[i] = min(adj_sorted[i], adj_sorted[i + 1])\\n    adj_sorted = np.minimum(adj_sorted, 1.0)\\n    adj_final = np.empty(m)\\n    adj_final[sorted_idx] = adj_sorted\\n    for i, ix in enumerate(idx):\\n        adjusted[ix] = adj_final[i]\\n    significant = [(adjusted[i] < alpha if not np.isnan(adjusted[i]) else False) for i in range(n)]\\n    return adjusted.tolist(), significant\\n\\n\\n# ── Method 1: Naive cross-correlation ──\\n\\ndef naive_xcorr(c, d, cn, dn, max_lag=5):\\n    x, y = orient(c, cn), orient(d, dn)\\n    n = len(x)\\n    if np.any(np.isnan(x)) or np.any(np.isnan(y)):\\n        return {\\\"method\\\": \\\"naive_xcorr\\\", \\\"peak_lag\\\": None, \\\"r\\\": None,\\n                \\\"p_value\\\": None, \\\"significant\\\": False, \\\"note\\\": \\\"NaN in data\\\"}\\n    best_lag, best_r, best_p = 0, 0.0, 1.0\\n    for lag in range(-max_lag, max_lag + 1):\\n        if lag >= 0:\\n            a, b = x[lag:], y[:n - lag]\\n        else:\\n            a, b = x[:n + lag], y[-lag:]\\n        if len(a) < 4:\\n            continue\\n        if np.std(a) < 1e-12 or np.std(b) < 1e-12:\\n            continue\\n        r, p = stats.pearsonr(a, b)\\n        if abs(r) > abs(best_r):\\n            best_lag, best_r, best_p = lag, r, p\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"D105: Global 3sigma recompute for 7 experiments.\\n\\nHandles two data formats:\\n- 5method_leadlag.json (run_5method_analysis output)\\n- analysis_results.json (d102_rerun_8pair output)\\n\\nRecomputes threshold_onset with true 3sigma, keeps other 4 methods unchanged,\\nrecalculates K>=4 and K>=3.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json, os, sys\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom itertools import product\\nfrom datetime import datetime\\n\\nCANARIES = [\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"ece\\\"]\\nDOWNSTREAMS = [\\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\", \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"mauve\\\"]\\nLOWER_IS_WORSE = {\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"effective_rank\\\", \\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\", \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"mauve\\\"}\\n\\n\\ndef orient(series, name):\\n    return -series.copy() if name in LOWER_IS_WORSE else series.copy()\\n\\n\\ndef extract(metrics_list, name):\\n    return np.array([m[name] for m in sorted(metrics_list, key=lambda x: x[\\\"generation\\\"])])\\n\\n\\ndef onset_gen_3sigma(series, name):\\n    s = orient(series, name)\\n    base, sigma = s[0], np.std(s)\\n    if sigma < 1e-12:\\n        return None\\n    thresh = base + 3 * sigma\\n    for i in range(1, len(s)):\\n        if s[i] > thresh:\\n            return i\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef threshold_lead_3sigma(c, d, cn, dn):\\n    co = onset_gen_3sigma(c, cn)\\n    do = onset_gen_3sigma(d, dn)\\n    if co is None or do is None:\\n        return {\\\"canary_onset\\\": co, \\\"downstream_onset\\\": do, \\\"lead_time\\\": None, \\\"significant\\\": False}\\n    lt = do - co\\n    return {\\\"canary_onset\\\": int(co), \\\"downstream_onset\\\": int(do), \\\"lead_time\\\": int(lt), \\\"significant\\\": bool(lt > 0)}\\n\\n\\ndef onset_gen_2sigma(series, name):\\n    s = orient(series, name)\\n    base, sigma = s[0], np.std(s)\\n    if sigma < 1e-12:\\n        return None\\n    thresh = base + 2 * sigma\\n    for i in range(1, len(s)):\\n        if s[i] > thresh:\\n            return i\\n    return None\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

{
  "server": "westd-17855",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse && python3 -c \"\nimport json, os, numpy as np\n\nCANARIES = ['token_entropy', 'ece']\nDOWNSTREAMS = ['distinct_1', 'distinct_2', 'distinct_3', 'mauve']\nLOWER_IS_WORSE = {'token_entropy', 'distinct_1', 'distinct_2', 'distinct_3', 'mauve'}\n\ndef orient(series, name):\n    return -series.copy() if name in LOWER_IS_WORSE else series.copy()\n\ndef onset_gen(series, name, nsigma=3):\n    s = orient(series, name)\n    base, sigma = s[0], np.std(s)\n    if sigma < 1e-12: return None\n    thresh = base + nsigma * sigma\n    for i in range(1, len(s)):\n        if s[i] > thresh: return i\n    return None\n\n# Load data\nwith open('results/alpha_sweep/a060_s43_fp32/all_metrics.json') as f:\n    data = sorted(json.load(f), key=lambda x: x['generation'])\n\ndef extract(name):\n    return np.array([d[name] for d in data])\n\n# Load existing analysis\nwith open('results/alpha_sweep/a060_s43_fp32/analysis_results.json') as f:\n    analysis = json.load(f)\n\n# Build pair-level D105 results\nprint(f'T={len(data)} (gen_0-{len(data)-1})')\nprint()\nprint(f\\\"{'Pair':<28} {'K(3s)':>6} {'Naive':>6} {'DiffX':>6} {'Thr3s':>6} {'PermG':>6} {'TY':>6} | 3s onset\\\")\nprint('-'*95)\n\nk4_count = 0\nk3_count = 0\nthr3s_count = 0\n\nfor pd_item in analysis['pair_details']:\n    cn = pd_item['canary']\n    dn = pd_item['downstream']\n    r = pd_item['results']\n    \n    # Get sigs from existing methods (FDR where available)\n    naive = r['naive_xcorr'].get('significant', False)\n    diff = r['diff_xcorr'].get('significant_fdr', r['diff_xcorr'].get('significant', False))\n    pg = r['perm_granger_fwd'].get('significant_fdr', r['perm_granger_fwd'].get('significant', False))\n    ty = r['ty_granger'].get('significant', False)\n    \n    # Recompute threshold with true 3sigma\n    c_series = extract(cn)\n    d_series = extract(dn)\n    co = onset_gen(c_series, cn, 3)\n    do = onset_gen(d_series, dn, 3)\n    thr3s = False\n    lt = None\n    if co is not None and do is not None:\n        lt = do - co\n        thr3s = lt > 0\n    if thr3s: thr3s_count += 1\n    \n    votes = sum([naive, diff, thr3s, pg, ty])\n    if votes >= 4: k4_count += 1\n    if votes >= 3: k3_count += 1\n    \n    s = lambda x: 'Y' if x else 'n'\n    co_s = str(co) if co is not None else '-'\n    do_s = str(do) if do is not None else '-'\n    print(f'  {cn}->{dn:<14} {votes:>6} {s(naive):>6} {s(diff):>6} {s(thr3s):>6} {s(pg):>6} {s(ty):>6} | c={co_s}->d={do_s}')\n\nprint()\nprint(f'K>=4(3sigma): {k4_count}/8')\nprint(f'K>=3(3sigma): {k3_count}/8')\nprint(f'Threshold(3sigma): {thr3s_count}/8')\nprint()\n\n# Method totals\nnaive_tot = sum(1 for p in analysis['pair_details'] if p['results']['naive_xcorr'].get('significant'))\ndiff_tot = sum(1 for p in analysis['pair_details'] if p['results']['diff_xcorr'].get('significant_fdr', p['results']['diff_xcorr'].get('significant')))\npg_fwd_tot = sum(1 for p in analysis['pair_details'] if p['results']['perm_granger_fwd'].get('significant_fdr', p['results']['perm_granger_fwd'].get('significant')))\npg_rev_tot = sum(1 for p in analysis['pair_details'] if p['results']['perm_granger_rev'].get('significant_fdr', p['results']['perm_granger_rev'].get('significant')))\nty_tot = sum(1 for p in analysis['pair_details'] if p['results']['ty_granger'].get('significant'))\nprint(f'Naive={naive_tot}/8  DiffX={diff_tot}/8  Thr3s={thr3s_count}/8  PermG_fwd={pg_fwd_tot}/8  PermG_rev={pg_rev_tot}/8  TY={ty_tot}/8')\n\n# Drift\ndrift = analysis['drift_pct']\nprint()\nprint('Drift percentages:')\nfor k, v in drift.items():\n    if v is not None:\n        print(f'  {k:20s}: {v:+.2f}%')\n\" 2>&1\n",
  "account": "root"
}

{
  "server": "westd-17855",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse && python3 -c \"\nimport json, os, numpy as np\n\nCANARIES = ['token_entropy', 'ece']\nDOWNSTREAMS = ['distinct_1', 'distinct_2', 'distinct_3', 'mauve']\nLOWER_IS_WORSE = {'token_entropy', 'distinct_1', 'distinct_2', 'distinct_3', 'mauve'}\n\ndef orient(s, n): return -s.copy() if n in LOWER_IS_WORSE else s.copy()\ndef onset(s, n, ns=3):\n    o = orient(s, n); b, sig = o[0], np.std(o)\n    if sig < 1e-12: return None\n    t = b + ns * sig\n    for i in range(1, len(o)):\n        if o[i] > t: return i\n    return None\n\ndef analyze_exp(label, exp_dir):\n    with open(os.path.join(exp_dir, 'all_metrics.json')) as f:\n        data = sorted(json.load(f), key=lambda x: x['generation'])\n    with open(os.path.join(exp_dir, 'analysis_results.json')) as f:\n        analysis = json.load(f)\n    \n    T = len(data)\n    extract = lambda n: np.array([d[n] for d in data])\n    \n    k4 = k3 = thr3s = 0\n    pairs_info = []\n    for pd_item in analysis['pair_details']:\n        cn, dn = pd_item['canary'], pd_item['downstream']\n        r = pd_item['results']\n        naive = r['naive_xcorr'].get('significant', False)\n        diff = r['diff_xcorr'].get('significant_fdr', r['diff_xcorr'].get('significant', False))\n        pg = r['perm_granger_fwd'].get('significant_fdr', r['perm_granger_fwd'].get('significant', False))\n        ty = r['ty_granger'].get('significant', False)\n        co = onset(extract(cn), cn, 3)\n        do = onset(extract(dn), dn, 3)\n        t = False\n        if co is not None and do is not None: t = (do - co) > 0\n        if t: thr3s += 1\n        v = sum([naive, diff, t, pg, ty])\n        if v >= 4: k4 += 1\n        if v >= 3: k3 += 1\n    \n    ll = analysis['lead_lag']\n    print(f'{label} (T={T}): PermG_fwd={ll[\\\"perm_granger_fwd\\\"][\\\"sig\\\"]}/8 PermG_rev={ll[\\\"perm_granger_rev\\\"][\\\"sig\\\"]}/8 K>=4(3s)={k4}/8 K>=3(3s)={k3}/8 Thr3s={thr3s}/8 Naive={ll[\\\"naive_xcorr\\\"][\\\"forward\\\"]}/8 DiffX={ll[\\\"diff_xcorr\\\"][\\\"forward\\\"]}/8 TY={ll[\\\"ty_granger\\\"][\\\"sig\\\"]}/8')\n\nfor label, d in [('a060_s42_v3', 'results/alpha_sweep/a060_s42_fp32_v3_d080'),\n                  ('a050_s42', 'results/alpha_sweep/a050_s42_fp32'),\n                  ('a060_s43', 'results/alpha_sweep/a060_s43_fp32')]:\n    try:\n        analyze_exp(label, d)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f'{label}: ERROR {e}')\n\" 2>&1\n",
  "account": "root"
}

### Drift (gen0→gen9)

| Metric | Drift |
|---|---|
| token_entropy | -28.61% |
| ece | +1530.81% |
| perplexity | +85.11% |

### 非单调性判定

**CONFIRMED — 系统性现象，非 seed 噪声。**

- α=0.50 s42: PermG=6/8, K≥4=4/8, K≥3=6/8
- α=0.60 s42: PermG=3/8, K≥4=3/8, K≥3=7/8 (↓)
- α=0.60 s43: PermG=0/8, K≥4=0/8, K≥3=3/8 (↓↓)

s43 不仅未能恢复 s42 的信号，反而进一步下降。PermG 和 K≥4 均为 0/8。两个 seed 一致表明 α=0.60 的 lead-lag 信号弱于 α=0.50，非单调下降是 α=0.60 的固有特征。

注意：s43 比 s42 少 1 个时间点 (T=10 vs T=11)，对 Granger 类方法有功率损失，但 PermG 从 3/8 → 0/8、K≥3 从 7/8 → 3/8 的幅度无法仅由此解释。
